# One Word Substitution
> Total Questions: 2031

---

[Q1] High-flown and pretentious
[EXAM: SSC MTS 09/10/2024 (Morning)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) bombastic
(b) austere
(c) schema ti sing
(d) mnemonic
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Bombastic- high-flown and pretentious. Austere- severe or strict in manner or attitude. Schema ti sing- organizing or arranging something according to a particular scheme or systematic plan. Mnemonic- a memory aid for something, often taking the form of a rhyme or an acronym.
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: दिए गए वाक्यांश के लिए उपयुक्त एक शब्द (One Word Substitute) 'bombastic' (आडंबरपूर्ण / दिखावटी (High-flown and pretentious)) है।

📝 **विकल्पों का अर्थ (Options)**: (A) bombastic: आडंबरपूर्ण / दिखावटी (High-flown and pretentious) • (B) austere: कठोर / सादा • (C) schema ti sing • (D) mnemonic: स्मृति-सहायक

[Q2] The monuments set up for people who were buried in other countries
[EXAM: SSC MTS 14/10/2024 (Morning)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) graves
(b) cemetery
(c) cenotaphs
(d) coffins
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Cenotaph- the monuments set up for people who were buried in other countries. 
• Grave - a hole dug in the ground to receive a coffin or dead body, typically marked by a stone or mound. Cemetery- an area of ground in which dead bodies are buried. Coffin- a long, narrow box, typically of wood, in which a dead body is buried or cremated.
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: दिए गए वाक्यांश के लिए उपयुक्त एक शब्द (One Word Substitute) 'cenotaphs' (स्मारकों) है।

📝 **विकल्पों का अर्थ (Options)**: (A) graves • (B) cemetery: कब्रिस्तान • (C) cenotaphs: स्मारकों • (D) coffins

[Q3] Exactly the same words as were used originally
[EXAM: SSC MTS 16/10/2024 (Evening)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Factual
(b) Ornate
(c) Precise
(d) Verbatim
[ANS] d
[SOL]
Verbatim- exactly the same words as were used originally. Factual- based on or containing facts. Ornate- having a lot of complicated decorations. Precise- marked by exactness and accuracy of expression or detail.
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: दिए गए वाक्यांश के लिए उपयुक्त एक शब्द (One Word Substitute) 'Verbatim' (प्रतिशब्द) है।

📝 **विकल्पों का अर्थ (Options)**: (A) Factual: वास्तविक • (B) Ornate: सरूप • (C) Precise: सटीक • (D) Verbatim: प्रतिशब्द

[Q4] A room in a hospital used especially for sunbathing or therapeutic exposure to light
[EXAM: SSC MTS 17/10/2024 (Morning)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Sanatorium
(b) Solarium
(c) Planetarium
(d) Podium
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Solarium- a room in a hospital used especially for sunbathing or therapeutic exposure to light. Sanatorium- an establishment for the medical treatment of people who are convalescing or have a chronic illness. Planetarium- a building specially equipped to show images of the stars in the sky and other objects in space on a curved ceiling. Podium- a raised area on which a person stands to speak to a large number of people.
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: दिए गए वाक्यांश के लिए उपयुक्त एक शब्द (One Word Substitute) 'Solarium' (धूपघर / शीशे का कमरा) है।

📝 **विकल्पों का अर्थ (Options)**: (A) Sanatorium: सेहतगाह • (B) Solarium: धूपघर / शीशे का कमरा • (C) Planetarium: नक्षत्र-भवन • (D) Podium: मंच

[Q6] The analysis of a person
[EXAM: SSC MTS 18/10/2024 (Afternoon)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Numismatics
(b) Graphology
(c) Philology
(d) Calligraphy
[ANS] b
[SOL]

[Q7] The branch of geography that studies mountains and their form at i on
[EXAM: SSC MTS 18/10/2024 (Afternoon)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Palaeo graph y
(b) Orthography
(c) Orography
(d) Myth o graph y
[ANS] c
[SOL]
Orography - the branch of geography that studies mountains and their formation. Palaeo graph y- the study of ancient writing systems and scripts, including the analysis of handwriting to read old texts. Orthography- the art of writing words with the proper letters according to standard usage. Myth o graph y- the representation of myths in art.
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: दिए गए वाक्यांश के लिए उपयुक्त एक शब्द (One Word Substitute) 'Orography' (ओरोग्राफी) है।

📝 **विकल्पों का अर्थ (Options)**: (A) Palaeo graph y • (B) Orthography: इमला • (C) Orography: ओरोग्राफी • (D) Myth o graph y

[Q8] A policy that separates people on the basis of race
[EXAM: SSC MTS 29/10/2024 (Evening)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Apostasy
(b) Apartheid
(c) Apostle
(d) Apiary
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Apartheid- a policy that separates people on the basis of race. Apostasy- the act of giving up your religious or political beliefs and leaving a religion or a political party. Apostle- one of the twelve disciples chosen by Christ to preach his gospel. Apiary- a place where bees are kept.

[Q13] A serpent having many heads in Greek mythology
[EXAM: SSC MTS 13/11/2024 (Evening)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Hydra
(b) Python
(c) Cobra
(d) Eopsin
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Hydra- a serpent having many heads in Greek mythology. Python- a large heavy non-venomous snake that kills its prey by constricting them. 
• Cobra - a kind of poisonous snake that can make the skin on the back of its neck into a hood. 
• Eopsin - the goddess of wealth and storage in Korean mythology.
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: दिए गए वाक्यांश के लिए उपयुक्त एक शब्द (One Word Substitute) 'Hydra' (हीड्रा) है।

[Q18] One who does not show response to good or bad
[EXAM: SSC CGL 09/09/2024 (2 n d Shift)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Stoic
(b) Sceptic
(c) Cynic
(d) Phobic
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Stoic- one who does not show response to good or bad. Sceptic- a person inclined to question or doubt accepted opinions. [ANS] a
[SOL]
Foreword- a short section (as of a book) that leads to or explains the main part. Conclusion- the end or finish of an event, process, or text. Postscript- a short remark or message added to the bottom of a letter after you have signed your name, usually introduced by the abbreviation PS. Paraphrase- express the meaning of (something written or spoken) using different words, especially to achieve greater clarity.
💡 हिन्दी व्याख्या: दिए गए वाक्यांश के लिए उपयुक्त एक शब्द (One Word Substitute) 'Foreword' (प्रस्तावना) है।

📝 **विकल्पों का अर्थ (Options)**: (A) Foreword: प्रस्तावना • (B) Conclusion • (C) Postscript • (D) Paraphrase

(d) Difficulty

(c) enzyme

उदा. 'Omniscient' का अर्थ सर्वज्ञ यानी सब कुछ जानने वाला होता है।
[RULE] Root 'Omni' (all) + 'scient' (knowing/wise).

[Q700] A list of books available in a library

[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) Catalogue
(b) Bibliography
(c) Lexicon
(d) Directory
[ANS] a
[SOL]
A catalogue is a complete list of items, such as books in a library.
उदा. 'Catalogue' पुस्तकालय में उपलब्ध पुस्तकों की एक व्यवस्थित सूची होती है।

(b) Monarchy
(c) Oligarchy
(d) Anarchy
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Monarchy is a form of government with a monarch (king or queen) at the head.

[RULE] Root 'Mon' (single) + 'archy' (rule).

[Q702] Words inscribed on a tomb

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Epitaph
(b) Epilogue
(c) Autograph
(d) Transcript
[ANS] a
[SOL]
An epitaph is a phrase or form of words written in memory of a person who has died, especially as an inscription on a tombstone.
उदा. 'Epitaph' कब्र या समाधि पर लिखे जाने वाले लेख को कहते हैं।
[RULE] Prefix 'Epi-' (upon) + 'taphios' (tomb).

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Plagiarism
(b) Piracy
(c) Theft
(d) Forgery
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Plagiarism is the practice of taking someone else's work or ideas and passing them off as one's own.
उदा. 'Plagiarism' किसी अन्य लेखक के विचारों या लेखों की चोरी करके उन्हें अपने नाम से प्रस्तुत करना है।
[RULE] Derived from Latin word 'plagiarius' (kidnapper).

(a) Ambivert

(c) Ambigraph
(d) Ambiguous
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Ambidextrous describes someone able to use the right and left hands equally well.
उदा. Ambidextrous उस व्यक्ति को कहते हैं जो अपने दोनों हाथों का इस्तेमाल समान रूप से कर सकता है।
[RULE] Root: 'Ambi' means both, 'dextrous' means skillful.

[Q716] a person who has the ability to understand and share the feelings of others
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Altruist
(b) Empathic
(c) Empath
(d) Philanthropist
[ANS] c
[SOL]
The phrase describes someone who feels or understands the emotions of others – an "empath".  Altruist and philanthropist refer to self‑lessness, while empathic is an adjective.
उदाहरण: She is an empath, understanding everyone's pain.
[RULE] One‑word substitution for a descriptive phrase

[Q717] a person who is knowledgeable about many different subjects
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Polymath
(b) Expert
(c) Specialist
(d) Guru
[ANS] a
[SOL]
A polymath has knowledge across various fields.  Expert and specialist denote depth in one area, and guru is a revered teacher.
उदाहरण: He is a true polymath, fluent in science, art, and literature.
[RULE] One‑word substitution for a long descriptive phrase

[Q718] A person who enjoys making jokes, especially in a condescending way
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Sarcastic
(b) Satirist
(c) Joker
(d) Parodist
[ANS] b
[SOL]
A satirist uses humor to ridicule and criticize.  Sarcastic is an adjective, joker a general comedian, and parodist imitates.
उदाहरण: He is a renowned satirist, mocking politics with wit.
[RULE] One‑word substitution for a descriptive phrase

[Q719] a person who takes pleasure in confusing others by using double meanings
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Ambiguity
(b) Wordplay
(c) Puzzler
(d) Eccentric
[ANS] b

Wordplay refers to playful use of language, often with double meanings.  Ambiguity is the quality, puzzler is a person who puzzles, eccentric is odd.
उदाहरण: The writer’s wordplay delighted readers.
[RULE] One‑word substitution for a long descriptive phrase

[Q720] a person who is excessively fond of eating sweets
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Sugary
(b) Sweetsman
(c) Glutton
(d) Cakey
[ANS] c
[SOL]
A glutton is someone who overindulges in food, especially sweets.  Sugary is an adjective, sweetsman is not standard, cakey is adjective.
उदाहरण: He is a glutton for desserts, never turning down cake.
[RULE] One‑word substitution for a long descriptive phrase

(a) Philosopher

(d) Ethicist

[RULE] One‑word substitution for a long descriptive phrase

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Museum
(b) Library
(c) Archive
(d) Sanctuary
[ANS] c
[SOL]
An archive is a collection of historical documents or records providing information about a place, institution, or group of people.
उदा. वह स्थान जहाँ सरकारी या ऐतिहासिक रिकॉर्ड रखे जाते हैं, 'Archive' (अभिलेखागार) कहलाता है।
[RULE] Standard archival vocabulary

[Q791] Study of birds
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XII (2024)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Entomology
(b) Ornithology
(c) Etymology
(d) Philology
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Ornithology is the scientific study of birds.
उदा. पक्षियों के वैज्ञानिक अध्ययन को 'Ornithology' (पक्षी विज्ञान) कहा जाता है।
[RULE] Root: Ornith (bird) + ology (study of)

(a) Ineligible
(b) Incorrigible
(c) Incomprehensible
(d) Indelible
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Incorrigible describes a bad habit or person that is not able to be changed or reformed.

(a) Misanthrope
(b) Philanthropist

(d) Polyglot
[ANS] b
[SOL]
A philanthropist seeks to promote the welfare of others, especially by the generous donation of money to good causes.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Etymology

(d) Linguistics
[ANS] a

Etymology is the study of the historical development of words and their meanings.

[RULE] Root: 'Etymon' (true sense) + 'logy' (study)

[Q795] A government by a small group of people
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Democracy
(b) Oligarchy
(c) Autocracy
(d) Plutocracy
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Oligarchy is a form of government in which a small group of people holds all power.
उदा. Oligarchy (अल्पतंत्र) वह शासन प्रणाली है जिसमें कुछ ही लोगों के हाथ में सत्ता होती है।

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Bilingual

[ANS] c

A polyglot is someone who knows or is able to use several languages.

[RULE] Root: 'Poly' (many) + 'glotta' (tongue/language)

[ANS] b

A theist is a person who believes in the existence of a god or gods.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Stoic

[ANS] a

A stoic is a person who can endure pain or hardship without showing their feelings or complaining.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Tribute
(b) Eulogy

(d) Monologue
[ANS] b
[SOL]
A eulogy is a speech or piece of writing that praises someone highly, typically someone who has just died. 
उदा. 'Eulogy' किसी की अत्यधिक प्रशंसा में दिया गया भाषण होता है, विशेषकर किसी की मृत्यु के बाद।
[RULE] Root word: 'Eu' means good/well.

[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) Bibliophile

(d) Lexicographer
[ANS] a
[SOL]
A bibliophile is a person who collects or has a great love of books.

[RULE] Root word: 'Biblio' means book, 'phile' means lover.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Democracy

[ANS] b

Oligarchy is a small group of people having control of a country or organization.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]

[Q807] The committee's decision was __________, which caused a lot of confusion.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2019]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) confusing
(b) confusion
(c) confusingly
(d) confused
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: Use an adjective to describe a decision that is unclear or confusing.
उदा. हिंदी में: "कमीटि का निर्णय बहुत भ्रमित करने वाला था," इसलिए सही शब्द "confusing" है।
[RULE] Adjective usage (describing noun)

[Q808] She was known for her __________, which made her a favorite among the students.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2019]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) charismatic
(b) charisma
(c) charismaticly
(d) charmer
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: Use an adjective that means possessing charm or attractiveness.
उदा. हिंदी में: "वह अपने आकर्षक स्वभाव के लिए जानी जाती थीं," अतः "charismatic" सही विकल्प है।
[RULE] Adjective (describing person)

[Q809] The report highlighted the __________ of the new policy.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) implication
(b) implicatory
(c) implicationally
(d) implicate
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: Use a noun that means the consequence or effect of something.
उदा. हिंदी में: "रिपोर्ट ने नई नीति के नतीजे पर प्रकाश डाला," अतः "implication" सही है।
[RULE] Noun (consequence)

[Q810] The meeting was __________, lasting more than four hours.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2019]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) protracted
(b) protraction
(c) protractively
(d) protract
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: Use an adjective meaning extended or drawn out.

[RULE] Adjective (extended duration)

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) pioneering
(b) pioneer

(d) pioneeringly
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: Use an adjective that means being first or innovative.

[RULE] Adjective (innovative)

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) significant

Rule in English: Use an adjective that describes the magnitude of an effect.

[RULE] Adjective (magnitude)

[Q813] The manager's approach was __________, leading to better team work

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) collaborative

[ANS] a

Rule in English: Use an adjective meaning working together or in partnership.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) vibrant

[ANS] a

Rule in English: Use an adjective that describes lively or full of life.

[EXAM: SSC CGL 2019]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) streamlined

[ANS] a

Rule in English: Use an adjective meaning organized, efficient, and smooth.

[RULE] Adjective (efficient)

[Q816] The athlete's performance was __________, breaking several records.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2019]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) spectacular
(b) spectacle
(c) spectacularly
(d) spectacularity
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: Use an adjective meaning impressive or outstanding.
उदा. हिंदी में: "खिलाड़ी का प्रदर्शन शानदार था," अतः "spectacular" सही विकल्प है।
[RULE] Adjective (impressive)

[Q817] The book offers a __________ perspective on climate change.
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2019]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) comprehensive
(b) comprehension
(c) comprehensively
(d) comprehender
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule in English: Use an adjective meaning covering all or nearly all elements.
उदा. हिंदी में: "पुस्तक में जलवायु परिवर्तन पर व्यापक दृष्टिकोण है," अतः "comprehensive" सही है।
[RULE] Adjective (complete)

[Q818] An inscription on a tombstone written in memory of the person who has died.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2020]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Epitaph
(b) Epilogue
(c) Autograph
(d) Eulogy
[ANS] a
[SOL]
An 'Epitaph' is a phrase or form of words written in memory of a person who has died, especially as an inscription on a tombstone. 
उदा. 'Epitaph' कब्र पर या किसी मृत व्यक्ति की याद में लिखा जाने वाला लेख (समाधि-लेख) होता है।
[RULE] Root: Epi (upon) + taphos (tomb)

[Q819] One who can use both hands equally well.

[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) Ambivert
(b) Ambidextrous
(c) Audacious
(d) Amorphous
[ANS] b
[SOL]
An 'Ambidextrous' person is able to use the right and left hands equally well. 
उदा. 'Ambidextrous' (उभयहस्त कुशल) वह व्यक्ति है जो अपने दोनों हाथों का समान रूप से उपयोग कर सकता है।
[RULE] Root: Ambi (both) + dexter (right/favorable)

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Extempore
(b) Dialogue
(c) Monologue
(d) Prologue
[ANS] a
[SOL]
An 'Extempore' speech is spoken or done without preparation. 
उदा. 'Extempore' (आशुभाषण) उस भाषण या प्रस्तुति को कहते हैं जो बिना किसी पूर्व तैयारी के दिया जाए।
[RULE] Latin root: Ex tempore (out of the time)

[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) Biography

[ANS] b

An 'Autobiography' is an account of a person's life written by that person.

[RULE] Root: Auto (self) + bio (life) + graphy (writing)

[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) Apiary

[ANS] b

[Q823] Fear of heights.

[Q824] A person who easily believes others

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Credible
(b) Credulous

(d) Fictitious

[RULE] Root: Cred (to believe) + -ous (full of)

[DIFFICULTY: Easy]
(a) Entomology
(b) Etymology
(c) Ecology
(d) Anthropology
[ANS] a
[SOL]
'Entomology' is the branch of zoology concerned with the study of insects.

[RULE] Root: Entomon (insect) + -logy (study of)

[Q826] A hater of mankind
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Misanthrope
(b) Philanthropist
(c) Misogynist
(d) Egotist
[ANS] a
[SOL]
A 'Misanthrope' is a person who dislikes humankind and avoids human society.

[RULE] Root: Mis (hater) + anthropos (mankind)

[Q827] State of growth between boyhood and youth
[EXAM: SSC CHSL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Adolescence
(b) Infancy
(c) Puberty
(d) Senility
[ANS] a

'Adolescence' is the period following the onset of puberty during which a young person develops from a child into an adult.

[Q828] The committee was determined to ___ the project within the next quarter.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) accelerate
(b) deliberate
(c) mitigate
(d) obfuscate
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The verb "accelerate" means to speed up or hasten. It fits the context of completing a project quickly.
उदाहरण: "वे परियोजना को जल्दी पूरा करने के लिए कार्य को तेज़ करना चाहते थे।"
[RULE] One-Word Substitution for Long Descriptions

[Q829] in spite of
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) despite
(b) because
(c) in addition to
(d) due to
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The preposition "despite" indicates contrast, exactly matching "in spite of".
उदाहरण: "उसकी अनुभवहीनता के बावजूद, उसने सफलता पाई।"
[RULE] One-Word Substitution for Phrases

[Q830] The new policy will ___ the current regulations.
[EXAM: SSC CPO 2023]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) conform
(b) contravene
(c) finalize
(d) reiterate
[ANS] a
[SOL]
To "conform" means to comply with or align with. It suits the idea of aligning the policy with existing regulations.

[ANS] a

[Q1000] An official pardon granted to a political offender

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Parole
(b) Amnesty
(c) Absolution
(d) Immunity
[ANS] b
[SOL]

उदा. 'Amnesty' का अर्थ राजनीतिक अपराधियों को दी जाने वाली आम क्षमा (राजक्षमा) होता है।
[RULE] Root: A- (not/without) + mnasthai (to remember)

[SOL]

उदा. जो व्यक्ति अपना कर्ज (debt) चुकाने में असमर्थ हो, उसे दिवालिया (Bankrupt) कहते हैं।
[RULE] Root word: 'rupt' (to break/burst) related to financial breaking.

(c) Borrower
(d) Lender
[ANS] b
[SOL]
A 'bankrupt' (or insolvent) is a person declared in law as unable to pay their debts. 
उदा. 'Bankrupt' (दिवालिया) उस व्यक्ति को कहते हैं जो अपना कर्ज चुकाने में असमर्थ हो।
[RULE] Root: Bank- + ruptus (broken)

[RULE] One-word for 'not enough'

[Q1186] in the event that

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) provided
(b) if
(c) unless
(d) thereby
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The phrase ‘in the event that’ means ‘if’. It introduces a conditional clause.
उदा. अगर बारिश होती है तो हम घर से बाहर नहीं निकलते।
[RULE] Conditional Conjunction

[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) however
(b) whether
(c) although
(d) though
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The phrase ‘be it that’ is used to introduce a clause that could be true or false, meaning ‘whether’. It is often used in formal writing.
उदा. चाहे वह सही हो या गलत, हमें निर्णय लेना है।
[RULE] Subordinating Conjunction

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]

(c) to

[ANS] a
[SOL]
The phrase ‘as far as I am concerned’ expresses a personal viewpoint, which can be replaced by the adverb ‘subjectively’.
उदा. व्यक्तिगत रूप से, मुझे यह योजना पसंद नहीं है।
[RULE] Adverbial Phrase

---

[Q1189] in spite of the fact that
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) despite
(b) although
(c) because
(d) while
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The phrase ‘in spite of the fact that’ indicates contrast, which can be expressed by the single word ‘despite’.
उदा. बावजूद इसके, वह समय पर पहुँचा।
[RULE] Conjunction of Contrast

---

[Q1190] at the present moment
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) currently
(b) therefore
(c) momentarily
(d) later
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The phrase ‘at the present moment’ can be replaced by the adverb ‘currently’ to indicate the present time.
उदा. वर्तमान में, परियोजना चल रही है।
[RULE] Adverbial Time

---

[Q1191] with all due respect
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Nefarious
(b) deferentially
(c) respectfully
(d) Obsolete
[ANS] c
[SOL]
The phrase ‘with all due respect’ is a polite preface, which can be expressed by the adverb ‘respectfully’.
उदा. सम्मानपूर्वक, मैं सहमत नहीं हूँ।
[RULE] Polite Address

---

[Q1192] in the course of
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) during
(b) by
(c) after
(d) before
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The phrase ‘in the course of’ indicates an action taking place over time and can be replaced by the preposition ‘during’.
उदा. उसके दौरान, वह अस्थायी रूप से अनुपस्थित रहा।
[RULE] Prepositional Phrase

---

[Q1193] in order to
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) so
(b) because
(c) for
(d) although
[ANS] c
[SOL]
The phrase ‘in order to’ expresses purpose and can be replaced by the preposition ‘for’.
उदा. यह परियोजना सफलता के लिए है।
[RULE] Purpose Preposition

---

[Q1194] to the best of my knowledge
[EXAM: SSC CGL 2024 Tier-1]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) as far as I know
(b) to the best
(c) to the extent
(d) to the best of
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The phrase ‘to the best of my knowledge’ means ‘as far as I know’ and can be replaced by the single phrase ‘as far as I know’.
उदा. मेरी जानकारी के अनुसार, वह अभी भी नौकरी पर है।
[RULE] Adverbial Clause

[Q1233] Someone who is extremely eager or enthusiastic about some thing.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XI (2023)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Avid
(b) Apathetic
(c) Indifferent
(d) Disinterested
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The adjective *avid* originates from Latin *avidus* meaning eager or greedy. It is used to describe a person with great enthusiasm.
उदाहरण: वह पुस्तक के प्रति बहुत ही उत्साही (avid) है।
[RULE] Word formation from Latin root

[Q1234] A person who is deeply interested in a particular subject or activity.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XI (2023)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Enthusiast
(b) Professional
(c) Novice
(d) Spectator
[ANS] a
[SOL]
An *enthusiast* is someone who is passionately involved in a subject. It comes from the Greek *enthousiasmos* meaning to be filled with a divine spirit.
उदाहरण: वह संगीत का एक उत्साही (enthusiast) है।
[RULE] Borrowed from Greek with suffix -ist

[Q1235] An act or instance of lending money to someone else for a period of time at a specified rate of interest.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XI (2023)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Loan
(b) Borrowing
(c) Mortgage
(d) Debt
[ANS] b
[SOL]
The noun *borrowing* refers to the act of taking and giving back money. It is derived from the verb *borrow*.
उदाहरण: बैंक से ऋण (borrowing) लेना आम बात है।
[RULE] Gerund forming noun from verb

[Q1236] A person who is highly skilled and experienced in a particular domain.
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XI (2023)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Expert
(b) Novice
(c) Amateur
(d) Beginner
[ANS] a
[SOL]
*Expert* means a person who has a superior knowledge or skill in a specific area. The word comes from Latin *expertus* meaning experienced.
उदाहरण: वह गणित में एक माहिर (expert) है।
[RULE] Word formation from Latin root

[Q1237] A person who has a strong sense of personal responsibility and is self-reliant.

*Self-reliant* describes someone who relies on themselves. It is formed by combining the prefix *self-* with *reliant*.

[Q1337] provided that

[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIII (2025)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) though
(b) despite
(c) although
(d) regardless
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Rule: Phrases like "in spite of" can be replaced by a single word that conveys the same contrast. "Despite" is the correct substitute.
उदाहरण: Despite the rain, the event continued.
[RULE] One-Word Substitution

---

[Q1539] in order to
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIII (2025)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) so
(b) so that
(c) in
(d) to
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Rule: The purpose expressed by "in order to" is best captured by the conjunction "so that".
उदाहरण: I study so that I can pass the exam.
[RULE] One-Word Substitution

---

[Q1540] for the purpose of
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIII (2025)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) for
(b) to
(c) in
(d) by
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: "For the purpose of" can be shortened to the single word "for", which retains the intended meaning.
उदाहरण: For the purpose of clarity, we will rephrase the sentence.
[RULE] One-Word Substitution

---

[Q1541] in other words
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIII (2025)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) simply
(b) otherwise
(c) else
(d) Munificent
[ANS] a
[SOL]
Rule: The phrase "in other words" is best expressed by the single word "simply".
उदाहरण: Simply put, the results are inconclusive.
[RULE] One-Word Substitution

---

[Q1542] in light of
[EXAM: SSC Selection Post Phase XIII (2025)]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) due to
(b) given
(c) because of
(d) as
[ANS] b
[SOL]
Rule: "In light of" means "considering" or "given". The one-word equivalent is "given".
उदाहरण: Given the circumstances, we will postpone the meeting.
[RULE] One-Word Substitution

उदा. Hindi: "समाचार बहुत डरावना था" → "समाचार बहुत डरावना था" (terrifying).

[Q1593] In the event of
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2026]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) If
(b) During
(c) While
(d) When
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The phrase "In the event of" can be replaced by a single word that conveys the same conditional meaning. The most common replacement is "If".
उदाहरण के लिये: "In the event of an emergency, we must act immediately." → "If an emergency occurs, we must act immediately."
[RULE] One-Word Substitution for Phrases

[Q1594] In spite of
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2026]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Despite
(b) Because
(c) But
(d) Although
[ANS] a
[SOL]
To express a contrast or concession, the phrase "In spite of" is often substituted with the single word "Despite".
उदाहरण के लिये: "In spite of the rain, the match continued." → "Despite the rain, the match continued."
[RULE] One-Word Substitution for Phrases

[Q1595] In order to
[EXAM: SSC Stenographer 2026]
[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) To
(b) For
(c) In
(d) At
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The purpose‑expressing phrase "In order to" can be simplified to the single word "To".
उदाहरण के लिये: "In order to finish on time, we worked all night." → "To finish on time, we worked all night."
[RULE] One-Word Substitution for Phrases

---

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) Except
(b) Excepting
(c) Unless
(d) Excepted
[ANS] a
[SOL]
The phrase "With the exception of" can be replaced by the single word "Except" to convey the same meaning.
उदाहरण के लिये: "With the exception of John, everyone was present." → "Except John, everyone was present."
[RULE] One-Word Substitution for Phrases

[RULE] One-Word Substitution for Phrases

(c) apathy

[ANS] a

[Q1886] The committee was _________ to approve the new policy.

[DIFFICULTY: Medium]
(a) sardonic
(b) adjourned
(c) ambivalent
(d) apprehensive
[ANS] d
[SOL]
Rule in English: The sentence describes hesitation or fear of approval. "Apprehensive" captures that feeling. "Sardonic" means mocking, "adjourned" is past tense of meeting delay, and "ambivalent" means having mixed feelings.
उदा. Hindi: "समिति नई नीति को मंजूरी देने से डर रही थी।"
[RULE] Adjective substitution for hesitation

[ANS] b
[SOL]

[ANS] d
[SOL]

उदा. Hindi: "उसने असुविधा के लिए दिल से माफी मांगी।"
[RULE] Adjective substitution for sincerity

[SOL]
Rule in English: The sentence indicates opposition. "Antithetical" means directly opposed. The other options denote agreement, sameness, or a two-part division.

[RULE] Adjective substitution for opposition

[RULE] One Word Substitution

[RULE] Word choice: Use a single adjective that conveys a long description.
